- **AI vs HPC:** storage. Large real SSD field-failure corpus with predictive features.

### Dataset of SSD Failures in Alibaba (Tianchi / FAST'21)
*URL:* tianchi.aliyun.com/dataset/152661
- **Contents:** ~1M SSDs, 11 models; SMART + tickets + physical location + workload tags (built for correlated-failure study).
- **Failure info:** failure tickets + location/app context for correlated/cascading analysis.
- **Access:** public (Tianchi registration).
- **AI vs HPC:** storage. Unique for how failures cluster/propagate.

### Large-scale Disk Failure Prediction Dataset (Tianchi / PAKDD 2020 Alibaba AIOps)
*URL:* tianchi.aliyun.com/dataset/70251
- **Contents:** multi-year daily SMART for a large HDD fleet with failure tags.
- **Failure info:** per-disk failure labels (strongly class-imbalanced).
- **Access:** public (Tianchi registration).
- **AI vs HPC:** storage. HDD counterpart to the SSD sets; classic imbalanced failure-prediction benchmark.

### TOKIO / pytokio + NERSC Cori Darshan+LMT corpus
*URL:* github.com/NERSC/pytokio ; arXiv:2204.08180
- **Contents:** framework joining Darshan job I/O with server-side Lustre LMT telemetry (5s sampling); ~1.1M Cori jobs.
- **Failure info:** mostly performance/congestion telemetry, not hard failure labels.
- **Access:** pytokio code public; full LMT/Darshan archives largely on-request from NERSC.
- **AI vs HPC:** HPC. Best cross-layer HPC I/O telemetry for congestion/slowdown.

### I/O Traces of HPC Applications (Frontera / Zenodo) ⭐
*DOI:* 10.5281/zenodo.17428437
- **Contents:** JSON I/O traces + dstat + NVIDIA-GPU metrics for GROMACS, OpenFOAM, PyTorch, TensorFlow on Frontera (~2.8 GB), Oct 2025.
- **Failure info:** none (behavioral I/O/telemetry only).
- **Access:** public download, CC-BY-4.0.
- **AI vs HPC:** both. Rare dataset placing traditional-HPC apps and AI frameworks on the same system.

*Lower-relevance I/O (workload only, no failure labels):* IO500 lists (io500.org/the-lists), MSR Cambridge block traces (iotta.snia.org/traces/388), FIU home/mail/web traces.

## Dataset repositories / archives not yet named

### IEEE DataPort
*aka:* ieee-dataport.org
*Org:* IEEE
*System:* umbrella archive
*Period:* ongoing
*Scale:* many datasets
- **Contents:** Hosts independently authored datasets across domains, including HPC log and failure sets.
- **Failure info:** REPOSITORY. Not itself a failure dataset; members carry their own provenance and verdicts.
- **Access:** mixed public / member-gated
- **Used by:** Member datasets appear separately where relevant.
- **AI vs HPC:** both
- **Caveats:** Some members are subscription-gated.

### OSTI.GOV
*aka:* osti.gov; DOE technical report index
*Org:* US Department of Energy
*System:* umbrella archive
*Period:* ongoing
*Scale:* DOE-wide
- **Contents:** Primary index for DOE laboratory technical reports, including RAS and reliability studies.
- **Failure info:** REPOSITORY. An index of reports; individual reports carry their own verdicts.
- **Access:** public
- **Used by:** Source for the Frontier, Lassen and TSUBAME reports in this catalog.
- **AI vs HPC:** HPC
- **Caveats:** Index only.

### OpsPAI / Tsinghua NetMan AIOps dataset hubs
*aka:* awesome-AIOps; nkcs.iops.ai
*Org:* OpsPAI / Tsinghua NetMan
*System:* umbrella archive
*Period:* ongoing
*Scale:* many datasets
- **Contents:** Curated lists pointing to failure, anomaly and disk-failure-prediction datasets held elsewhere.
- **Failure info:** REPOSITORY. A list of pointers, not a dataset.
- **Access:** public
- **Used by:** Not cited in the paper.
- **AI vs HPC:** both
- **Caveats:** Adjacent to HPC/AI ops rather than specific to it.

### Chameleon Trovi artifact sharing
*aka:* chameleoncloud.org/experiment/share
*Org:* Chameleon testbed
*System:* umbrella archive
*Period:* ongoing
*Scale:* many artifacts
- **Contents:** Artifact-sharing service for reproducible systems experiments on GPU and NVMe testbeds.
- **Failure info:** REPOSITORY. Members carry their own provenance.
- **Access:** public
- **Used by:** Not cited in the paper.
- **AI vs HPC:** both
- **Caveats:** Testbed experiments rather than production failure data.

## Confirmed NOT to exist as downloadable data (agents checked)

No public logbooks from Cohere/Mistral/Character.ai; no standalone public DKRZ Mistral I/O raw trace; no standalone public TSUBAME/MareNostrum/Piz Daint/JUWELS/Aurora/Sierra RAS release; no distinct Tencent SDC field study or open GPU-Xid raw log dataset. Shockwave, Bamboo, Oobleck, Gemini, ByteScale are recovery/scheduler systems that consume existing traces and release no new failure data.

## Highest-value new pulls for the baseline

SURF Lisa (ML-vs-generic on one machine with exit labels), NREL Eagle/Kestrel (clean labeled Slurm outcomes at scale), ALCF Cobalt+RAS (job outcome tied to RAS), and on the AI side Unicron / L4 / Meta FT-HSDP / 504-B200 for normalized failure rates.
